  <script src="https://unpkg.com/peerjs@1.5.2/dist/peerjs.min.js"></script>
  <script>
    (function () {
      'use strict';

      const canvas = document.getElementById('game');
      const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d');

      const V_W = 1000, V_H = 1000, V_CX = 500, V_CY = 500, V_ARENA_R = 380;
      const MAX_PLAYERS = 6;
      const PEER_PREFIX = 'pithu-game-';

      const cfg = {
        playerR: 20, ballR: 8,
        humanSpeed: 280,
        defenderSpeedByDiff: { easy: 180, normal: 230, hard: 280 },
        attackerThrowSpeed: 600, attackerThrowCooldown: 0.45,
        defenderThrowSpeedByDiff: { easy: 320, normal: 420, hard: 520 },
        defenderThrowCooldownByDiff: { easy: 1.6, normal: 1.15, hard: 0.75 },
        humanDefThrowSpeed: 500, humanDefThrowCooldown: 0.9,
        aimErrorByDiff: { easy: 0.42, normal: 0.2, hard: 0.06 },
        dodgeMultiplier: 2.1, dodgeDuration: 0.22, dodgeCooldown: 1.3,
        stonesTotal: 7,
        guardRadius: 80, aiThrowRangeMul: 1.3
      };

      const COLORS = { attacker: '#E8A23D', defender: '#1B6E6A' };

      function rand(a, b) { return a + Math.random() * (b - a); }
      function dist(x1, y1, x2, y2) { return Math.hypot(x1 - x2, y1 - y2); }
      function lerp(a, b, t) { return a + (b - a) * t; }

      function generateShortCode() {
        const chars = 'ABCDEFGHJKLMNPQRSTUVWXYZ23456789';
        let res = '';
        for (let i = 0; i < 5; i++) res += chars.charAt(Math.floor(Math.random() * chars.length));
        return res;
      }

      const S = {
        screen: 'menu', mode: null, currentRoomCode: null,
        roomConfig: { rounds: 3, lives: 1, diff: 'normal' },
        phase: 'aim', attackingTeam: 'A',
        scores: { A: 0, B: 0 }, round: 1, winnerTeam: null,
        muted: false,
        players: {}, playerOrder: ['p1'],
        balls: [], stones: [], placedCount: 0,
        particles: [], shake: { t: 0, mag: 0 },
        lastTime: 0, time: 0,
        toastTimer: null,
        peer: null, conn: null, conns: {}, isOnline: false, netRole: 'host', netTimer: 0,
        myId: 'p1', matchStarted: false,
        audioQueue: [], toastQueue: [],
        isTutorial: false
      };

      function newPlayer(id, label) {
        return {
          id, label, team: null, isAI: false, isOut: false, lives: 1,
          x: 0, y: 0, r: cfg.playerR, facing: { x: 0, y: -1 }, carrying: null,
          invulnT: 0, dodgeT: 0, dodgeCdT: 0, throwCdT: 0, hitFlashT: 0,
          controls: { up: false, down: false, left: false, right: false, joyDx: 0, joyDy: 0 }
        };
      }

      function initPlayers() { S.players.p1 = newPlayer('p1', 'P1'); }

      function activePlayers() { return S.playerOrder.map(id => S.players[id]).filter(Boolean); }
      function getAttackers() { return activePlayers().filter(p => p.team === S.attackingTeam); }
      function getDefenders() { return activePlayers().filter(p => p.team && p.team !== S.attackingTeam); }
      function otherTeam(t) { return t === 'A' ? 'B' : 'A'; }
      function myTeam() {
        if (S.mode === 'single') return 'A';
        const id = S.netRole === 'client' ? S.myId : 'p1';
        return (id && S.players[id]) ? S.players[id].team : null;
      }
      function teamLabel(team) {
        if (S.mode === 'single') return team === 'A' ? 'You' : 'CPU';
        return team === myTeam() ? 'Your team' : 'Team ' + team;
      }
      function getLocalPlayer() {
        if (S.mode === 'single') return S.players.p1;
        if (S.netRole === 'host') return S.players.p1;
        if (S.netRole === 'client') return S.myId ? (S.players[S.myId] || null) : null;
        return S.players.p1 || null;
      }

      function canDefendersThrow() {
        return S.phase === 'rebuild' && !(S.stones || []).some(s => s.state === 'flying');
      }

      /* ---------- display sizing ---------- */
      function resize() {
        const w = window.innerWidth || 800; const h = window.innerHeight || 600;
        const dpr = Math.min(2, window.devicePixelRatio || 1);
        canvas.width = Math.round(w * dpr); canvas.height = Math.round(h * dpr);
      }
      window.addEventListener('resize', resize);

      /* ---------- audio ---------- */
      let actx = null;
      function ensureAudio() {
        if (!actx) { try { actx = new (window.AudioContext || window.webkitAudioContext)(); } catch (e) { } }
        if (actx && actx.state === 'suspended') { actx.resume(); }
      }
      function beep(freq, dur, type, gainStart) {
        if (S.muted || !actx) return;
        try {
          const o = actx.createOscillator(); const g = actx.createGain();
          o.type = type || 'sine'; o.frequency.value = freq;
          g.gain.value = gainStart || 0.15;
          g.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.0001, actx.currentTime + dur);
          o.connect(g); g.connect(actx.destination);
          o.start(); o.stop(actx.currentTime + dur);
        } catch (e) { }
      }
      function playSound(name) {
        if (S.isOnline && S.netRole === 'host') S.audioQueue.push(name);
        if (!actx) return;
        switch (name) {
          case 'throw': beep(320, 0.12, 'triangle', 0.12); break;
          case 'knock': beep(140, 0.35, 'sawtooth', 0.2); beep(90, 0.4, 'square', 0.12); break;
          case 'block': beep(500, 0.08, 'square', 0.1); break;
          case 'hit': beep(120, 0.25, 'sawtooth', 0.22); break;
          case 'pick': beep(660, 0.08, 'sine', 0.1); break;
          case 'place': beep(500, 0.1, 'triangle', 0.14); break;
          case 'dash': beep(800, 0.08, 'sine', 0.08); break;
          case 'win': beep(660, 0.15, 'triangle', 0.18); setTimeout(() => beep(880, 0.25, 'triangle', 0.18), 140); break;
          case 'lose': beep(200, 0.3, 'sawtooth', 0.18); break;
        }
      }

      /* ---------- Tutorial Engine ---------- */
      function startTutorial() {
        S.isTutorial = true;
        S.roomConfig.diff = 'easy'; 
        S.roomConfig.lives = 5; 
        S.roomConfig.rounds = 2; 
        document.getElementById('tutorial-banner').style.display = 'block';
        startMatch('single');
      }

      function updateTutorialDisplay() {
        if (!S.isTutorial) return;
        const banner = document.getElementById('tutorial-banner');
        const isAttacker = S.players.p1 && S.players.p1.team === S.attackingTeam;
        
        if (isAttacker) {
          if (S.phase === 'aim') {
            banner.innerHTML = "<b>ATTACKER PHASE</b>Move using the Joystick / WASD.<br>Face the stone stack and press ACTION to throw the ball.";
          } else {
            banner.innerHTML = "<b>REBUILD PHASE</b>Walk over scattered stones to pick them up, then walk to the center to rebuild.<br>Press ACTION to dodge if the CPU throws the ball at you!";
          }
        } else {
          if (S.phase === 'aim') {
            banner.innerHTML = "<b>DEFENDER PHASE</b>Wait for the attacker to throw the ball and break the stack.";
          } else {
            banner.innerHTML = "<b>DEFEND THE STACK</b>Face the attacker and press ACTION to throw the ball at them!<br>Stop them from rebuilding the 7 stones.";
          }
        }
      }

      /* ---------- match logic ---------- */
      function makeStack() {
        const arr = [];
        for (let i = 0; i < 7; i++) {
          arr.push({ i, state: 'stacked', x: V_CX, y: V_CY - i * 8, z: 0, rot: rand(-0.15, 0.15), landed: true, placedIndex: null, claimedBy: null });
        }
        return arr;
      }
      function knockStack() {
        playSound('knock'); shake(14, 0.4);
        const baseAngle = rand(0, Math.PI * 2);
        (S.stones || []).forEach((s, idx) => {
          const angle = baseAngle + (idx / 7) * Math.PI * 2 + rand(-0.3, 0.3);
          const r = rand(V_ARENA_R * 0.28, V_ARENA_R * 0.78);
          s.state = 'flying'; s.from = { x: V_CX, y: V_CY - idx * 8 };
          s.to = { x: V_CX + Math.cos(angle) * r, y: V_CY + Math.sin(angle) * r };
          s.x = s.from.x; s.y = s.from.y; s.maxZ = rand(40, 90); s.z = 0;
          s.animT = 0; s.animDur = rand(0.35, 0.6); s.landed = false; s.claimedBy = null;
        });
        S.phase = 'rebuild';
        if(S.isTutorial) updateTutorialDisplay();
      }
      function updateStones(dt) {
        (S.stones || []).forEach(s => {
          if (s.state === 'flying') {
            s.animT += dt; const t = Math.min(1, s.animT / s.animDur);
            const e = 1 - Math.pow(1 - t, 3);
            s.x = lerp(s.from.x, s.to.x, e); s.y = lerp(s.from.y, s.to.y, e);
            s.z = Math.sin(t * Math.PI) * s.maxZ;
            if (t >= 1) { s.state = 'scattered'; s.landed = true; s.z = 0; spawnDust(s.x, s.y, 6); }
          }
        });
      }
      function updatePickupDeposit() {
        if (S.phase !== 'rebuild') return;
        getAttackers().forEach(atk => {
          if (atk.isOut) return;
          if (atk.carrying == null) {
            for (const s of (S.stones || [])) {
              if (s.state === 'scattered' && s.landed && !s.claimedBy && dist(atk.x, atk.y, s.x, s.y) < atk.r + 14) {
                s.state = 'carried'; s.claimedBy = atk.id; atk.carrying = s.i; playSound('pick'); break;
              }
            }
          } else {
            const s = (S.stones || []).find(st => st.i === atk.carrying);
            if (!s) { atk.carrying = null; return; }
            if (dist(atk.x, atk.y, V_CX, V_CY) < 40) {
              s.state = 'placed'; s.placedIndex = S.placedCount; S.placedCount++;
              atk.carrying = null; playSound('place'); shake(5, 0.15); spawnDust(V_CX, V_CY, 8);
              if (S.placedCount >= cfg.stonesTotal) { endRound('attackers'); }
            } else {
              s.x = atk.x; s.y = atk.y - 30;
            }
          }
        });
      }

      function spawnDust(x, y, count) {
        for (let i = 0; i < (count || 8); i++) {
          S.particles.push({ x, y, vx: rand(-80, 80), vy: rand(-80, 80), life: 0, maxLife: rand(0.3, 0.6), r: rand(3, 6) });
        }
      }
      function updateParticles(dt) {
        for (let i = (S.particles || []).length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
          const p = S.particles[i];
          p.life += dt; p.x += p.vx * dt; p.y += p.vy * dt; p.vx *= 0.9; p.vy *= 0.9;
          if (p.life >= p.maxLife) S.particles.splice(i, 1);
        }
      }
      function shake(mag, t) { S.shake = { mag: mag || 0, t: t || 0 }; }

      /* ---------- Combat & Hit Detection ---------- */
      function throwBall(player, speed, cooldown) {
        if (!player) return;
        player.throwCdT = cooldown;
        const dx = player.facing.x;
        const dy = player.facing.y;
        S.balls.push({
          x: player.x + dx * (player.r + 12), y: player.y + dy * (player.r + 12),
          vx: dx * speed, vy: dy * speed, r: cfg.ballR, owner: player.id, history: []
        });
        playSound('throw');
      }

      function handleAction(player) {
        if (S.screen !== 'playing' || !player) return;
        const isAttacker = player.team === S.attackingTeam;

        if (isAttacker) {
          if (player.isOut) return;

          if (S.phase === 'aim') {
            if (player.throwCdT <= 0 && S.balls.length === 0) {
              throwBall(player, cfg.attackerThrowSpeed, cfg.attackerThrowCooldown);
            }
          } else if (S.phase === 'rebuild') {
            if (player.dodgeCdT <= 0) {
              player.dodgeT = cfg.dodgeDuration; player.dodgeCdT = cfg.dodgeCooldown; playSound('dash');
            }
          }
        } else {
          if (canDefendersThrow() && player.throwCdT <= 0) {
            const speed = player.isAI ? cfg.defenderThrowSpeedByDiff[S.roomConfig.diff] : cfg.humanDefThrowSpeed;
            const cd = player.isAI ? cfg.defenderThrowCooldownByDiff[S.roomConfig.diff] : cfg.humanDefThrowCooldown;
            throwBall(player, speed, cd);
          }
        }
      }

      function flashHit(p) { if (p) p.hitFlashT = 0.3; }

      function updateBalls(dt) {
        for (let i = (S.balls || []).length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
          const b = S.balls[i]; if (!b) continue;
          b.history = b.history || []; b.history.push({ x: b.x, y: b.y });
          if (b.history.length > 6) b.history.shift();
          b.x += (b.vx || 0) * dt; b.y += (b.vy || 0) * dt;
          let remove = false;
          if (dist(b.x, b.y, V_CX, V_CY) > V_ARENA_R + 80) remove = true;

          if (!remove && b.owner !== 'neutral') {
            const thrower = S.players[b.owner];
            const throwerTeam = thrower ? thrower.team : null;
            const opponents = activePlayers();

            for (let oi = 0; oi < opponents.length; oi++) {
              const target = opponents[oi];
              if (target.id === b.owner || target.team === throwerTeam) continue;
              if (dist(b.x, b.y, target.x, target.y) >= b.r + target.r) continue;

              if (target.team === S.attackingTeam) {
                if (!target.isOut && target.invulnT <= 0 && target.dodgeT <= 0) {
                  target.lives--;
                  playSound('hit'); shake(12, 0.35); flashHit(target); spawnDust(target.x, target.y, 12);

                  if (target.carrying != null) {
                    const s = (S.stones || []).find(st => st.i === target.carrying);
                    if (s) { s.state = 'scattered'; s.landed = true; s.claimedBy = null; }
                    target.carrying = null;
                  }

                  if (target.lives <= 0) {
                    target.isOut = true;
                    if (getAttackers().every(p => p.isOut)) endRound('defenders');
                  } else {
                    target.invulnT = 1.0;
                  }
                  remove = true;
                }
              } else {
                playSound('block');
                b.vx *= -0.4; b.vy *= -0.4; b.owner = 'neutral';
                spawnDust(b.x, b.y, 4);
              }
              break;
            }
            if (!remove && S.phase === 'aim' && thrower && thrower.team === S.attackingTeam) {
              if (dist(b.x, b.y, V_CX, V_CY) < b.r + 40) { knockStack(); remove = true; }
            }
          }
          if (remove) S.balls.splice(i, 1);
        }
      }

      /* ---------- movement ---------- */
      function clampToArena(p) {
        if (!p) return;
        const d = dist(p.x, p.y, V_CX, V_CY); const maxD = V_ARENA_R - p.r - 4;
        if (d > maxD && d > 0) { const k = maxD / d; p.x = V_CX + (p.x - V_CX) * k; p.y = V_CY + (p.y - V_CY) * k; }
      }
      function updateHuman(p, dt) {
        if (!p || !p.controls) return;
        if (p.team === S.attackingTeam && p.isOut) return;
        let dx = 0, dy = 0, isJoy = false;
        if (p.controls.joyDx || p.controls.joyDy) { dx = p.controls.joyDx; dy = p.controls.joyDy; isJoy = true; }
        else {
          if (p.controls.up) dy -= 1; if (p.controls.down) dy += 1;
          if (p.controls.left) dx -= 1; if (p.controls.right) dx += 1;
        }
        if (dx || dy) {
          const len = Math.hypot(dx, dy); const nx = dx / len, ny = dy / len; p.facing = { x: nx, y: ny };
          const speedMult = isJoy ? Math.min(len, 1) : 1;
          const spd = cfg.humanSpeed * (p.dodgeT > 0 ? cfg.dodgeMultiplier : 1) * speedMult;
          p.x += nx * spd * dt; p.y += ny * spd * dt;
          if (p.dodgeT > 0 && Math.random() < 0.4) spawnDust(p.x, p.y + p.r, 1);
        }
        clampToArena(p);
      }

      function updateAI(ai, dt) {
        if (!ai || (ai.team === S.attackingTeam && ai.isOut)) return;

        let tx = ai.x, ty = ai.y;

        if (ai.team === S.attackingTeam) {
          const myAtkIndex = getAttackers().indexOf(ai);

          if (S.phase === 'aim') {
            tx = ai.x; ty = ai.y; 
            const dx = V_CX - ai.x, dy = V_CY - ai.y;
            const distToStack = Math.hypot(dx, dy) || 1;
            ai.facing = { x: dx / distToStack, y: dy / distToStack };

            const humanAttackerAlive = getAttackers().some(p => !p.isAI && !p.isOut);
            if (!humanAttackerAlive && ai.throwCdT <= 0 && S.balls.length === 0) {
              if (Math.random() < 0.02 + (myAtkIndex === 0 ? 0.05 : 0)) {
                throwBall(ai, cfg.attackerThrowSpeed, cfg.attackerThrowCooldown);
              }
            }
          } else if (S.phase === 'rebuild') {
            if (ai.carrying == null) {
              const availableStones = (S.stones || []).filter(s => s.state === 'scattered' && (!s.claimedBy || s.claimedBy === ai.id));
              if (availableStones.length > 0) {
                const stone = availableStones[myAtkIndex % availableStones.length];
                tx = stone.x; ty = stone.y;
              } else { tx = V_CX; ty = V_CY; }
            } else {
              tx = V_CX; ty = V_CY; 
            }

            const dx = tx - ai.x, dy = ty - ai.y;
            const distToTarget = Math.hypot(dx, dy) || 1;
            if (distToTarget > 4) {
              ai.facing = { x: dx / distToTarget, y: dy / distToTarget };
            }
          }
        } else {
          const myDefIndex = getDefenders().indexOf(ai);

          if (S.phase === 'aim') {
            const defs = getDefenders();
            const n = Math.max(1, defs.length);
            const halfSpread = Math.min(0.8, 0.35 * (n - 1));
            const t = n === 1 ? 0 : (myDefIndex / (n - 1)) - 0.5;
            const angle = -Math.PI / 2 + t * halfSpread * 2;

            tx = V_CX + Math.cos(angle) * cfg.guardRadius;
            ty = V_CY + Math.sin(angle) * cfg.guardRadius;

            const dx = V_CX - ai.x, dy = V_CY - ai.y;
            const distToCenter = Math.hypot(dx, dy) || 1;
            ai.facing = { x: dx / distToCenter, y: dy / distToCenter };

          } else if (S.phase === 'rebuild') {
            const activeAttackers = getAttackers().filter(a => !a.isOut);
            const atk = activeAttackers[myDefIndex % activeAttackers.length] || activeAttackers[0];
            if (!atk) return;

            const dx = ai.x - atk.x, dy = ai.y - atk.y;
            const baseAng = Math.atan2(dy, dx);
            const spreadAng = baseAng + (myDefIndex % 2 === 0 ? 1 : -1) * Math.ceil(myDefIndex / 2) * 0.6;

            tx = atk.x + Math.cos(spreadAng) * 200;
            ty = atk.y + Math.sin(spreadAng) * 200;

            const fx = atk.x - ai.x, fy = atk.y - ai.y;
            const flen = Math.hypot(fx, fy) || 1;
            ai.facing = { x: fx / flen, y: fy / flen };

            if (canDefendersThrow() && ai.throwCdT <= 0 && flen < V_ARENA_R * cfg.aiThrowRangeMul) {
              const err = rand(-cfg.aimErrorByDiff[S.roomConfig.diff], cfg.aimErrorByDiff[S.roomConfig.diff]);
              const ang = Math.atan2(fy, fx) + err;
              ai.facing = { x: Math.cos(ang), y: Math.sin(ang) };
              throwBall(ai, cfg.defenderThrowSpeedByDiff[S.roomConfig.diff], cfg.defenderThrowCooldownByDiff[S.roomConfig.diff]);
            }
          }
        }

        const mx = tx - ai.x, my = ty - ai.y;
        const mlen = Math.hypot(mx, my);
        if (mlen > 4) {
          const speed = ai.team === S.attackingTeam ? 220 : (cfg.defenderSpeedByDiff[S.roomConfig.diff] || 150);
          ai.x += (mx / mlen) * speed * dt;
          ai.y += (my / mlen) * speed * dt;
        }
        clampToArena(ai);
      }

      /* ---------- team layout & rounds ---------- */
      function layoutTeam(list, atBottom) {
        const n = Math.max(1, list.length);
        const halfSpread = Math.min(0.95, 0.32 * (n - 1));
        list.forEach((p, i) => {
          const t = n === 1 ? 0 : (i / (n - 1)) - 0.5;
          const angle = (atBottom ? Math.PI / 2 : -Math.PI / 2) + t * halfSpread * 2;
          const rad = V_ARENA_R * 0.62;
          p.x = V_CX + Math.cos(angle) * rad; p.y = V_CY + Math.sin(angle) * rad;
          const fx = V_CX - p.x, fy = V_CY - p.y; const fl = Math.hypot(fx, fy) || 1;
          p.facing = { x: fx / fl, y: fy / fl };
        });
      }
      function startRound() {
        S.phase = 'aim'; S.placedCount = 0; S.balls = []; S.particles = [];
        activePlayers().forEach(p => {
          p.invulnT = 0; p.dodgeT = 0; p.dodgeCdT = 0; p.throwCdT = 0; p.hitFlashT = 0; p.carrying = null;
          p.isOut = false;
          p.lives = parseInt(S.roomConfig.lives) || 1;
        });
        layoutTeam(getAttackers(), true); layoutTeam(getDefenders(), false);
        S.stones = makeStack();
        showToast('Round ' + (S.round || 1) + ' — ' + teamLabel(S.attackingTeam) + ' attacking', 2.0);
        if(S.isTutorial) updateTutorialDisplay();
      }
      function endRound(winnerSide) {
        const winnerTeam = winnerSide === 'attackers' ? S.attackingTeam : otherTeam(S.attackingTeam);
        if (!S.scores) S.scores = { A: 0, B: 0 };
        S.scores[winnerTeam] = (S.scores[winnerTeam] || 0) + 1;
        playSound(winnerSide === 'attackers' ? 'win' : 'lose');
        updateHUD();

        const roundsNeeded = parseInt(S.roomConfig.rounds) || 3;
        if (S.scores[winnerTeam] >= roundsNeeded) {
          S.winnerTeam = winnerTeam; S.screen = 'gameOver'; showGameOver(winnerTeam);
        } else {
          S.screen = 'roundEnd';
          const msg = winnerSide === 'attackers' ? 'PITHU! ' + teamLabel(winnerTeam) + ' rebuilt the stack!' : teamLabel(winnerTeam) + ' wins the round!';
          showToast(msg, 2.2, () => {
            S.round = (S.round || 1) + 1;
            S.attackingTeam = otherTeam(S.attackingTeam); 
            startRound(); S.screen = 'playing';
          });
        }
      }

      /* ---------- UI & HUD ---------- */
      const toastEl = document.getElementById('toast');
      function showToast(text, duration, cb) {
        if (S.isOnline && S.netRole === 'host') S.toastQueue.push({ text, duration });
        toastEl.textContent = text; toastEl.classList.add('show');
        if (S.toastTimer) clearTimeout(S.toastTimer);
        S.toastTimer = setTimeout(() => { toastEl.classList.remove('show'); if (cb) cb(); }, duration * 1000);
      }

      function updateHUD() {
        try {
          document.getElementById('hud-round').textContent = 'Round ' + (S.round || 1);
          document.getElementById('score-p1').textContent = (S.scores && S.scores.A) || 0;
          document.getElementById('score-p2').textContent = (S.scores && S.scores.B) || 0;
          document.getElementById('name-p1').textContent = S.mode === 'single' ? 'You' : 'Team A';
          document.getElementById('name-p2').textContent = S.mode === 'single' ? 'CPU' : 'Team B';

          const atks = getAttackers();
          const totalLives = atks.reduce((sum, p) => sum + (p.isOut ? 0 : p.lives), 0);
          document.getElementById('hud-lives').textContent = totalLives > 0 ? '❤'.repeat(totalLives) : '—';

          document.getElementById('hud-progress').textContent = (S.placedCount || 0) + ' / ' + cfg.stonesTotal + ' stones';
          const hint = document.getElementById('hud-hint');
          if (S.phase === 'aim') hint.textContent = teamLabel(S.attackingTeam) + ' — face stack & press ACTION to throw';
          else hint.textContent = teamLabel(S.attackingTeam) + ': rebuild!  ' + teamLabel(otherTeam(S.attackingTeam)) + ': press ACTION to throw';

          updateRoster();
        } catch (e) { }
      }
      function updateRoster() {
        const box = document.getElementById('hud-roster'); if (!box) return;
        const list = (S.playerOrder && S.playerOrder.length) ? S.playerOrder : Object.keys(S.players);
        box.innerHTML = list.map(id => {
          const p = S.players[id]; if (!p) return '';
          const isMe = S.mode !== 'single' && ((S.netRole === 'host' && id === 'p1') || (S.netRole === 'client' && id === S.myId));
          const label = S.mode === 'single' ? (id === 'p1' ? 'You' : 'CPU') : (isMe ? 'You' : (p.label || id.toUpperCase()));
          const dotColor = p.team === S.attackingTeam ? 'var(--attacker)' : 'var(--defender)';
          const outAtk = (p.team === S.attackingTeam && p.isOut);
          const style = outAtk ? 'opacity:0.4;text-decoration:line-through;' : '';
          let livesVisual = '';
          if (p.team === S.attackingTeam && !p.isOut && parseInt(S.roomConfig.lives) > 1) {
            livesVisual = ` <span style="color:var(--gulal);font-size:10px;">${'❤'.repeat(p.lives)}</span>`;
          }
          return `<span style="display:inline-flex;align-items:center;gap:4px;padding:2px 8px;border-radius:999px;background:rgba(243,233,210,0.08);font-size:11px;${style}"><span style="width:7px;height:7px;border-radius:50%;background:${dotColor};display:inline-block;"></span>${label}${livesVisual}</span>`;
        }).join('');
      }

      /* ---------- drawing ---------- */
      function draw() {
        if (!canvas.width) resize();
        const w = canvas.width, h = canvas.height;
        ctx.setTransform(1, 0, 0, 1, 0, 0);
        ctx.fillStyle = '#171224'; ctx.fillRect(0, 0, w, h);

        let scale = Math.min(w / V_W, h / V_H) || 1;
        const offsetX = (w - V_W * scale) / 2, offsetY = (h - V_H * scale) / 2;

        ctx.save(); ctx.setTransform(scale, 0, 0, scale, offsetX, offsetY);
        if (S.shake && S.shake.t > 0) ctx.translate(rand(-1, 1) * S.shake.mag * S.shake.t, rand(-1, 1) * S.shake.mag * S.shake.t);

        drawArena(); drawStones(); drawPlayers(); drawBalls(); drawParticles();
        ctx.restore();
      }

      function drawArena() {
        const cg = ctx.createRadialGradient(V_CX, V_CY - V_ARENA_R * 0.3, 10, V_CX, V_CY, V_ARENA_R || 100);
        cg.addColorStop(0, '#C17A48'); cg.addColorStop(1, '#8C5027');
        ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(V_CX, V_CY, V_ARENA_R, 0, Math.PI * 2); ctx.fillStyle = cg; ctx.fill();

        ctx.setLineDash([10, 10]); ctx.lineWidth = 3; ctx.strokeStyle = 'rgba(243,233,210,0.55)';
        ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(V_CX, V_CY, V_ARENA_R, 0, Math.PI * 2); ctx.stroke(); ctx.setLineDash([]);
        ctx.strokeStyle = 'rgba(243,233,210,0.18)'; ctx.lineWidth = 2;
        ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(V_CX, V_CY, V_ARENA_R * 0.55, 0, Math.PI * 2); ctx.stroke();

        ctx.beginPath(); ctx.ellipse(V_CX, V_CY + 4, 40, 16, 0, 0, Math.PI * 2);
        ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(0,0,0,0.25)'; ctx.fill();
      }
      function drawStoneShape(x, y, rx, ry, rot, shade) {
        ctx.save(); ctx.translate(x, y); ctx.rotate(rot || 0);
        const grad = ctx.createLinearGradient(-(rx || 1), -(ry || 1), (rx || 1), (ry || 1));
        grad.addColorStop(0, shade[0]); grad.addColorStop(1, shade[1]);
        ctx.beginPath(); ctx.ellipse(0, 0, Math.max(1, rx || 1), Math.max(1, ry || 1), 0, 0, Math.PI * 2);
        ctx.fillStyle = grad; ctx.fill(); ctx.lineWidth = 1.5; ctx.strokeStyle = 'rgba(0,0,0,0.3)'; ctx.stroke();
        ctx.restore();
      }
      function drawStones() {
        const shadeStack = ['#F0B968', '#C77E22'], shadeGround = ['#E8CBA0', '#B98A52'];
        (S.stones || []).forEach(s => {
          if (!s) return;
          if (s.state === 'stacked' || s.state === 'placed') {
            const idx = s.state === 'placed' ? (s.placedIndex || 0) : (s.i || 0);
            drawStoneShape(V_CX, V_CY - idx * 8, 32 - idx * 1, 18 - idx * 0.5, s.rot || 0, shadeStack);
          } else if (s.state === 'flying') {
            ctx.beginPath(); ctx.ellipse(s.x || 0, (s.y || 0) + 10, 22, 10, 0, 0, Math.PI * 2);
            ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(0,0,0,0.25)'; ctx.fill();
            drawStoneShape(s.x || 0, (s.y || 0) - (s.z || 0), 24, 14, (s.rot || 0) + (s.animT || 0) * 10, shadeStack);
          } else if (s.state === 'scattered') {
            const pulse = 0.5 + 0.5 * Math.sin((S.time || 0) * 4 + (s.i || 0));
            ctx.beginPath(); ctx.ellipse(s.x || 0, (s.y || 0) + 10, 26, 10, 0, 0, Math.PI * 2);
            ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(0,0,0,0.2)'; ctx.fill();
            drawStoneShape(s.x || 0, s.y || 0, 24, 14, s.rot || 0, shadeGround);
          }
        });
      }
      function drawPlayers() {
        activePlayers().forEach(p => {
          if (!p || !isFinite(p.x)) return;
          const isOutAtk = (p.team === S.attackingTeam && p.isOut);
          ctx.save();
          if (isOutAtk) ctx.globalAlpha = 0.3;
          const color = p.team === S.attackingTeam ? COLORS.attacker : COLORS.defender;

          ctx.beginPath(); ctx.ellipse(p.x, p.y + p.r * 0.7, p.r * 0.9, p.r * 0.4, 0, 0, Math.PI * 2);
          ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(0,0,0,0.3)'; ctx.fill();
          if (p.carrying != null) drawStoneShape(p.x, p.y - 30, 18, 10, 0, ['#F0B968', '#C77E22']);

          ctx.save();
          if (p.invulnT > 0 || p.dodgeT > 0) ctx.globalAlpha = (isOutAtk ? 0.3 : 1) * (0.55 + 0.35 * Math.sin(S.time * 20));
          ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(p.x, p.y, p.r, 0, Math.PI * 2);
          ctx.fillStyle = (p.hitFlashT > 0) ? '#ffffff' : color; ctx.fill();
          ctx.lineWidth = 3; ctx.strokeStyle = 'rgba(23,18,36,0.8)'; ctx.stroke();
          ctx.restore();

          const fx = p.x + p.facing.x * (p.r + 10), fy = p.y + p.facing.y * (p.r + 10);
          ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(fx, fy, 5, 0, Math.PI * 2); ctx.fillStyle = '#F3E9D2'; ctx.fill();

          ctx.font = '700 16px Mukta, sans-serif'; ctx.fillStyle = '#F3E9D2'; ctx.textAlign = 'center';
          const isMe = S.mode !== 'single' && ((S.netRole === 'host' && p.id === 'p1') || (S.netRole === 'client' && p.id === S.myId));
          const label = S.mode === 'single' ? (p.id === 'p1' ? 'You' : 'CPU') : (isMe ? 'You' : (p.label || p.id.toUpperCase()));
          ctx.fillText(label + (isOutAtk ? ' (out)' : ''), p.x, p.y - p.r - 12);
          ctx.restore();
        });
      }
      function drawBalls() {
        (S.balls || []).forEach(b => {
          if (!b || !isFinite(b.x)) return;
          if (b.history && b.history.length > 0) {
            ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(b.history[0].x, b.history[0].y);
            for (let j = 1; j < b.history.length; j++) ctx.lineTo(b.history[j].x, b.history[j].y);
            ctx.strokeStyle = b.owner === 'neutral' ? 'rgba(255,255,255,0.4)' : ((S.players[b.owner] && S.players[b.owner].team === S.attackingTeam) ? 'rgba(232,162,61,0.5)' : 'rgba(27,110,106,0.5)');
            ctx.lineWidth = b.r * 1.5; ctx.lineCap = 'round'; ctx.lineJoin = 'round'; ctx.stroke();
          }
          ctx.beginPath(); ctx.ellipse(b.x, b.y + 12, b.r * 1.2, b.r * 0.6, 0, 0, Math.PI * 2);
          ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(0,0,0,0.15)'; ctx.fill();
          ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(b.x, b.y, b.r, 0, Math.PI * 2);
          const g = ctx.createRadialGradient(b.x - 2, b.y - 2, 1, b.x, b.y, b.r * 1.1);
          g.addColorStop(0, '#E58B94'); g.addColorStop(1, '#B23A48');
          ctx.fillStyle = g; ctx.fill();
        });
      }
      function drawParticles() {
        (S.particles || []).forEach(p => {
          let a = 1 - (p.life / p.maxLife); if (a < 0 || a > 1) return;
          ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(p.x, p.y, Math.max(0.1, p.r * a), 0, Math.PI * 2);
          ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(243,233,210,' + (a * 0.7) + ')'; ctx.fill();
        });
      }

      /* ---------- Inputs ---------- */
      const KEYMAP = { KeyW: 'up', ArrowUp: 'up', KeyS: 'down', ArrowDown: 'down', KeyA: 'left', ArrowLeft: 'left', KeyD: 'right', ArrowRight: 'right', Space: 'action', Enter: 'action' };

      function togglePause() {
        if (S.screen !== 'playing' && S.screen !== 'paused') return;

        if (S.isOnline && S.netRole === 'client') {
          const pauseOverlay = document.getElementById('overlay-pause');
          if (pauseOverlay.classList.contains('visible')) {
            pauseOverlay.classList.remove('visible');
          } else {
            showOverlay('overlay-pause');
            document.getElementById('btn-restart').style.display = 'none';
            document.getElementById('btn-mainmenu').textContent = 'Leave Match';
          }
        } else {
          if (S.screen === 'playing') {
            S.screen = 'paused';
            showOverlay('overlay-pause');
            document.getElementById('btn-restart').style.display = 'block';
            document.getElementById('btn-mainmenu').textContent = 'Main Menu';
          }
          else if (S.screen === 'paused') {
            S.screen = 'playing';
            hideOverlays();
          }
        }
      }

      window.addEventListener('keydown', e => {
        if (e.repeat) return;
        if (e.code === 'Escape') { togglePause(); return; }

        const pauseOverlay = document.getElementById('overlay-pause');
        if (pauseOverlay && pauseOverlay.classList.contains('visible')) return;

        if (S.screen !== 'playing') return;

        const k = KEYMAP[e.code]; if (!k) return;
        e.preventDefault(); ensureAudio();
        if (S.isOnline && S.netRole === 'client') {
          if (S.conn && S.conn.open) S.conn.send(k === 'action' ? { type: 'action' } : { type: 'input', key: k, val: true });
          return;
        }
        const lp = getLocalPlayer();
        if (lp) k === 'action' ? handleAction(lp) : lp.controls[k] = true;
      });

      window.addEventListener('keyup', e => {
        const k = KEYMAP[e.code]; if (!k || k === 'action') return;
        if (S.isOnline && S.netRole === 'client') { if (S.conn && S.conn.open) S.conn.send({ type: 'input', key: k, val: false }); return; }
        const lp = getLocalPlayer(); if (lp) lp.controls[k] = false;
      });

      function bindJoystick(zoneEl, knobEl) {
        let activeId = null, lastSend = 0;
        function updateJoy(ev) {
          if (activeId !== ev.pointerId) return;
          const pauseOverlay = document.getElementById('overlay-pause');
          if (pauseOverlay && pauseOverlay.classList.contains('visible')) return;

          const zRect = zoneEl.getBoundingClientRect(), zRadius = zRect.width / 2;
          let dx = ev.clientX - (zRect.left + zRadius), dy = ev.clientY - (zRect.top + zRadius);
          const d = Math.hypot(dx, dy); if (d > zRadius) { dx = (dx / d) * zRadius; dy = (dy / d) * zRadius; }
          knobEl.style.transform = `translate(calc(-50% + ${dx}px), calc(-50% + ${dy}px))`;
          const nx = dx / zRadius, ny = dy / zRadius;
          if (S.isOnline && S.netRole === 'client') {
            const now = performance.now();
            if (now - lastSend > 30 && S.conn && S.conn.open) { S.conn.send({ type: 'joy', x: nx, y: ny }); lastSend = now; }
          } else {
            const lp = getLocalPlayer(); if (lp) { lp.controls.joyDx = nx; lp.controls.joyDy = ny; }
          }
        }
        zoneEl.addEventListener('pointerdown', ev => { ev.preventDefault(); if (activeId !== null) return; activeId = ev.pointerId; try { zoneEl.setPointerCapture(activeId); } catch (e) { } updateJoy(ev); ensureAudio(); });
        zoneEl.addEventListener('pointermove', ev => { if (activeId === ev.pointerId) updateJoy(ev); });
        function stopJoy(ev) {
          if (activeId !== ev.pointerId) return;
          activeId = null; knobEl.style.transform = 'translate(-50%, -50%)';
          if (S.isOnline && S.netRole === 'client') { if (S.conn && S.conn.open) S.conn.send({ type: 'joy', x: 0, y: 0 }); }
          else { const lp = getLocalPlayer(); if (lp) { lp.controls.joyDx = 0; lp.controls.joyDy = 0; } }
        }
        zoneEl.addEventListener('pointerup', stopJoy); zoneEl.addEventListener('pointercancel', stopJoy);
      }
      function bindActionButton(el) {
        el.addEventListener('pointerdown', ev => {
          ev.preventDefault(); ensureAudio();

          const pauseOverlay = document.getElementById('overlay-pause');
          if (pauseOverlay && pauseOverlay.classList.contains('visible')) return;

          if (S.isOnline && S.netRole === 'client') { if (S.conn && S.conn.open) S.conn.send({ type: 'action' }); }
          else { const lp = getLocalPlayer(); if (lp) handleAction(lp); }
        });
      }

      /* ---------- Networking & Lobby Setup ---------- */
      function applyPlayerState(p, pSt) {
        if (!p || !pSt) return;
        Object.keys(pSt).forEach(k => { if (k !== 'facing' && k !== 'controls') p[k] = pSt[k]; });
        if (pSt.facing) { p.facing.x = pSt.facing.x; p.facing.y = pSt.facing.y; }
      }

      function showLobby() {
        showOverlay('overlay-lobby');
        document.getElementById('lobby-linkrow').style.display = 'flex';
        document.getElementById('lobby-code-display').textContent = S.currentRoomCode || '.....';

        const dis = S.netRole === 'client';
        ['rounds', 'lives', 'diff'].forEach(k => document.getElementById('config-' + k).disabled = dis);
        document.getElementById('settings-lock-msg').style.display = dis ? 'block' : 'none';
        renderLobbyPlayers();
      }

      function renderLobbyPlayers() {
        const listA = document.getElementById('team-a-list'), listB = document.getElementById('team-b-list');
        if (!listA || !listB) return;
        listA.innerHTML = ''; listB.innerHTML = '';

        S.playerOrder.forEach(id => {
          const p = S.players[id]; if (!p) return;
          const isMe = (S.netRole === 'host' && id === 'p1') || (S.netRole === 'client' && id === S.myId);
          const hostTag = id === 'p1' ? ' <span style="opacity:0.5; font-size:11px;">(Host)</span>' : '';
          const name = isMe ? '<b>You</b>' : (p.label || id.toUpperCase());

          const html = `<div class="lobby-player-row">
          <span>${name}${hostTag}</span>
          ${isMe ? `<button class="switch-team-btn" onclick="window.requestTeamSwitch()">Switch</button>` : ''}
        </div>`;
          if (p.team === 'A') listA.innerHTML += html;
          else listB.innerHTML += html;
        });

        const btnStart = document.getElementById('btn-start-game');
        if (btnStart) {
          const cA = Object.values(S.players).filter(p => p.team === 'A').length;
          const cB = Object.values(S.players).filter(p => p.team === 'B').length;
          btnStart.style.display = (S.netRole === 'host' && (cA + cB) >= 2) ? 'block' : 'none';
        }
        document.getElementById('lobby-status').textContent = S.netRole === 'host' ?
          (S.playerOrder.length + '/' + MAX_PLAYERS + ' joined — Need at least 2 to start.') :
          ('Waiting for host to start… (' + S.playerOrder.length + '/' + MAX_PLAYERS + ')');
      }

      function broadcastLobby() {
        const pData = {};
        S.playerOrder.forEach(id => {
          if (S.players[id]) pData[id] = { team: S.players[id].team };
        });
        const payload = { type: 'lobby', playerOrder: S.playerOrder, players: pData };
        Object.values(S.conns).forEach(c => { if (c && c.open) { try { c.send(payload); } catch (e) { } } });
      }

      function broadcastConfig() {
        if (S.netRole !== 'host') return;
        const payload = { type: 'config', config: S.roomConfig };
        Object.values(S.conns).forEach(c => { if (c && c.open) { try { c.send(payload); } catch (e) { } } });
      }

      window.requestTeamSwitch = function () {
        if (S.mode === 'single') return;
        if (S.netRole === 'host') {
          S.players['p1'].team = S.players['p1'].team === 'A' ? 'B' : 'A';
          renderLobbyPlayers(); broadcastLobby();
        } else if (S.netRole === 'client' && S.conn && S.conn.open) {
          S.conn.send({ type: 'switchTeam' });
        }
      };

      function removePlayerFromMatch(id) {
        delete S.conns[id]; const wasStarted = S.matchStarted;
        S.playerOrder = S.playerOrder.filter(x => x !== id); delete S.players[id];
        if (!wasStarted) { renderLobbyPlayers(); broadcastLobby(); return; }
        if (getAttackers().length === 0) endRound('defenders');
        else if (getDefenders().length === 0) endRound('attackers');
      }

      function setupHostConnection(c, playerId) {
        c.on('data', data => {
          try {
            const p = S.players[playerId]; if (!p) return;
            if (data.type === 'input') p.controls[data.key] = data.val;
            if (data.type === 'joy') { p.controls.joyDx = data.x; p.controls.joyDy = data.y; }
            if (data.type === 'action') handleAction(p);
            if (data.type === 'switchTeam' && !S.matchStarted) {
              p.team = p.team === 'A' ? 'B' : 'A';
              renderLobbyPlayers(); broadcastLobby();
            }
          } catch (e) { }
        });
        c.on('close', () => removePlayerFromMatch(playerId));
      }
      function setupClientConnection(c) {
        c.on('data', data => {
          try {
            if (data.type === 'assign') { S.myId = data.id; renderLobbyPlayers(); }
            else if (data.type === 'lobby') {
              S.playerOrder = data.playerOrder;
              S.playerOrder.forEach(id => {
                if (!S.players[id]) S.players[id] = newPlayer(id, id.toUpperCase());
                if (data.players && data.players[id]) {
                  S.players[id].team = data.players[id].team;
                }
              });
              renderLobbyPlayers();
            }
            else if (data.type === 'config') {
              S.roomConfig = data.config;
              ['rounds', 'lives', 'diff'].forEach(k => { document.getElementById('config-' + k).value = S.roomConfig[k]; });
            }
            else if (data.type === 'full') { document.getElementById('lobby-status').textContent = 'Room is full.'; }
            else if (data.type === 'state') {
              const st = data.state;

              const pauseOverlay = document.getElementById('overlay-pause');
              if (pauseOverlay && pauseOverlay.classList.contains('visible') && st.screen === 'playing') {
                // Let the client keep their pause menu open locally
              } else {
                if (st.screen === 'playing' && S.screen !== 'playing') {
                  S.screen = 'playing'; hideOverlays();
                  const tc = document.getElementById('touch-controls'); if (tc) tc.style.display = 'flex';
                } else if (st.screen === 'gameOver' && S.screen !== 'gameOver') {
                  S.screen = 'gameOver'; showGameOver(st.winnerTeam); 
                } else if (st.screen === 'roundEnd') S.screen = 'roundEnd';
              }

              S.phase = st.phase; S.attackingTeam = st.attackingTeam; S.scores = st.scores;
              S.round = st.round; S.placedCount = st.placedCount; S.shake = st.shake; S.winnerTeam = st.winnerTeam;
              if (st.playerOrder) S.playerOrder = st.playerOrder;
              if (st.players) {
                Object.keys(st.players).forEach(id => {
                  if (!S.players[id]) S.players[id] = newPlayer(id, id.toUpperCase());
                  applyPlayerState(S.players[id], st.players[id]);
                });
              }
              S.balls = st.balls || []; S.stones = st.stones || []; S.particles = st.particles || [];

              updateHUD();
            }
            if (data.audio) data.audio.forEach(a => playSound(a));
            if (data.toasts) data.toasts.forEach(t => showToast(t.text, t.duration));
          } catch (e) { }
        });
        c.on('close', () => { showToast('Connection lost', 3); setTimeout(backToMenu, 3000); });
      }

      function initOnlineHost(btnEl) {
        if (btnEl) btnEl.disabled = true;
        S.mode = 'multi'; S.isOnline = true; S.netRole = 'host'; S.myId = 'p1';
        S.playerOrder = ['p1']; if (!S.players.p1) S.players.p1 = newPlayer('p1', 'P1');
        S.players.p1.team = 'A';

        S.currentRoomCode = generateShortCode();

        showLobby();
        document.getElementById('lobby-status').textContent = 'Creating room...';

        S.peer = new Peer(PEER_PREFIX + S.currentRoomCode);
        S.peer.on('open', id => {
          document.getElementById('lobby-code-display').textContent = S.currentRoomCode;
          renderLobbyPlayers();
        });

        S.peer.on('error', err => {
          document.getElementById('lobby-status').textContent = 'Network Error. Refresh and try again.';
          if (btnEl) btnEl.disabled = false;
        });

        S.peer.on('connection', connection => {
          if (S.matchStarted || S.playerOrder.length >= MAX_PLAYERS) {
            connection.on('open', () => { try { connection.send({ type: 'full' }); } catch (e) { } }); return;
          }
          const newId = 'p' + (S.playerOrder.length + 1);
          S.playerOrder.push(newId);
          S.players[newId] = newPlayer(newId, newId.toUpperCase());

          const cA = Object.values(S.players).filter(p => p.team === 'A').length;
          const cB = Object.values(S.players).filter(p => p.team === 'B').length;
          S.players[newId].team = (cA <= cB) ? 'A' : 'B';

          S.conns[newId] = connection; setupHostConnection(connection, newId);
          connection.on('open', () => {
            try { connection.send({ type: 'assign', id: newId }); } catch (e) { }
            broadcastConfig(); renderLobbyPlayers(); broadcastLobby();
            showToast(newId.toUpperCase() + ' joined!', 2);
          });
        });
      }

      function initOnlineClient(roomCode) {
        S.mode = 'multi'; S.isOnline = true; S.netRole = 'client'; S.myId = null;
        S.playerOrder = ['p1']; if (!S.players.p1) S.players.p1 = newPlayer('p1', 'P1');

        S.currentRoomCode = roomCode; 

        showLobby();
        document.getElementById('lobby-status').textContent = 'Connecting to Room ' + roomCode + '...';

        S.peer = new Peer();
        S.peer.on('open', () => {
          S.conn = S.peer.connect(PEER_PREFIX + roomCode, { reliable: true });
          setupClientConnection(S.conn);
        });
        S.peer.on('error', () => {
          document.getElementById('lobby-status').textContent = 'Invalid Room Code or Host unavailable.';
        });
      }

      function showOverlay(id) {
        document.querySelectorAll('.overlay').forEach(o => o.classList.remove('visible'));
        document.getElementById(id).classList.add('visible');
      }
      function hideOverlays() { document.querySelectorAll('.overlay').forEach(o => o.classList.remove('visible')); }

      function showGameOver(winnerTeam) {
        S.isTutorial = false;
        document.getElementById('tutorial-banner').style.display = 'none';
        
        document.getElementById('gameover-title').textContent = S.mode === 'single' ? (winnerTeam === 'A' ? 'You win!' : 'CPU wins') : (winnerTeam === myTeam() ? 'Your team wins!' : 'Team ' + winnerTeam + ' wins!');
        document.getElementById('gameover-sub').textContent = 'Final score — Team A ' + ((S.scores && S.scores.A) || 0) + ' · Team B ' + ((S.scores && S.scores.B) || 0);

        const btnPlayAgain = document.getElementById('btn-playagain');
        const btnMainMenu = document.getElementById('btn-mainmenu2');

        if (S.mode === 'multi' && S.netRole === 'client') {
          btnPlayAgain.style.display = 'none';
          btnMainMenu.textContent = 'Leave Match';
        } else {
          btnPlayAgain.style.display = 'block';
          btnPlayAgain.textContent = 'Restart Match';
          btnMainMenu.textContent = 'Main Menu';
        }

        showOverlay('overlay-gameover');
      }

      function startMatch(mode) {
        ensureAudio(); S.mode = mode; S.scores = { A: 0, B: 0 }; S.round = 1;

        S.playerOrder = S.playerOrder.filter(id => {
          if (S.players[id] && S.players[id].isAI) {
            delete S.players[id];
            return false;
          }
          return true;
        });

        if (mode === 'single') {
          if(!S.isTutorial) S.roomConfig.lives = 2; 
          if (!S.playerOrder.includes('p1')) S.playerOrder.push('p1');
          if (!S.playerOrder.includes('p2')) S.playerOrder.push('p2');
          if (!S.players.p2) S.players.p2 = newPlayer('p2', 'CPU');
          S.players.p1.team = 'A'; S.players.p2.team = 'B'; S.players.p2.isAI = true; S.attackingTeam = 'A';
        } else {
          let cA = 0, cB = 0;
          S.playerOrder.forEach(id => {
            if (S.players[id]) {
              S.players[id].isAI = false;
              if (S.players[id].team === 'A') cA++; else cB++;
            }
          });

          if (cA === 0 || cB === 0) {
            const emptyTeam = cA === 0 ? 'A' : 'B';
            const numBots = Math.max(cA, cB);
            for (let i = 0; i < numBots; i++) {
              const botId = 'cpu_' + emptyTeam + '_' + i;
              S.playerOrder.push(botId);
              S.players[botId] = newPlayer(botId, 'CPU ' + (i + 1));
              S.players[botId].team = emptyTeam;
              S.players[botId].isAI = true;
            }
          }
        }
        hideOverlays();
        const tc = document.getElementById('touch-controls'); if (tc) tc.style.display = 'flex';
        startRound(); S.screen = 'playing'; updateHUD();
      }
      function backToMenu() { window.location.hash = ''; window.location.reload(); }

      function buildNetworkState() {
        const playersOut = {};
        S.playerOrder.forEach(id => {
          const p = S.players[id]; if (!p) return;
          playersOut[id] = { x: p.x, y: p.y, r: p.r, facing: p.facing, team: p.team, isOut: p.isOut, isAI: p.isAI, carrying: p.carrying, hitFlashT: p.hitFlashT, dodgeT: p.dodgeT, invulnT: p.invulnT, label: p.label, lives: p.lives };
        });
        return {
          phase: S.phase, attackingTeam: S.attackingTeam, scores: S.scores, round: S.round, placedCount: S.placedCount, shake: S.shake, winnerTeam: S.winnerTeam, screen: S.screen, playerOrder: S.playerOrder, players: playersOut,
          balls: (S.balls || []).map(b => ({ x: b.x, y: b.y, r: b.r, owner: b.owner, history: b.history ? b.history.slice(-5) : [] })),
          stones: (S.stones || []).map(s => ({ i: s.i, state: s.state, x: s.x, y: s.y, z: s.z, rot: s.rot, landed: s.landed, placedIndex: s.placedIndex, animT: s.animT })),
          particles: (S.particles || []).map(p => ({ x: p.x, y: p.y, r: p.r, life: p.life, maxLife: p.maxLife }))
        };
      }

      function loop(ts) {
        requestAnimationFrame(loop);
        if (!S.lastTime) S.lastTime = ts; const dt = Math.min(0.033, (ts - S.lastTime) / 1000 || 0);
        S.lastTime = ts; S.time += dt;

        if (S.screen === 'playing') {
          if (!S.isOnline || S.netRole === 'host') {
            activePlayers().forEach(p => {
              p.throwCdT = Math.max(0, p.throwCdT - dt); p.dodgeCdT = Math.max(0, p.dodgeCdT - dt);
              p.dodgeT = Math.max(0, p.dodgeT - dt); p.invulnT = Math.max(0, p.invulnT - dt); p.hitFlashT = Math.max(0, p.hitFlashT - dt);
              if (p.isAI) updateAI(p, dt); else updateHuman(p, dt);
            });
            updateStones(dt); updatePickupDeposit(); updateBalls(dt); updateParticles(dt);
            if (S.shake && S.shake.t > 0) S.shake.t = Math.max(0, S.shake.t - dt);
            updateHUD();

            if (S.isOnline && S.netRole === 'host') {
              S.netTimer += dt;
              if (S.netTimer > 0.033) {
                S.netTimer = 0; const payload = { type: 'state', state: buildNetworkState(), audio: S.audioQueue, toasts: S.toastQueue };
                Object.values(S.conns).forEach(c => { if (c && c.open) { try { c.send(payload); } catch (e) { } } });
                S.audioQueue = []; S.toastQueue = [];
              }
            }
          }
        }
        draw();
      }

      document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', () => {
        initPlayers(); resize();

        if (!localStorage.getItem('pithu_tutorial_completed')) {
          hideOverlays();
          showOverlay('overlay-tutorial');
        } else {
          hideOverlays();
          showOverlay('overlay-menu');
        }

        document.getElementById('btn-start-tut').addEventListener('click', () => {
          localStorage.setItem('pithu_tutorial_completed', 'true');
          startTutorial();
        });
        
        document.getElementById('btn-skip-tut').addEventListener('click', () => {
          localStorage.setItem('pithu_tutorial_completed', 'true');
          hideOverlays();
          showOverlay('overlay-menu');
        });

        // Config Listeners
        ['rounds', 'lives', 'diff'].forEach(k => {
          document.getElementById('config-' + k).addEventListener('change', (e) => {
            if (S.netRole === 'host') { S.roomConfig[k] = e.target.value; broadcastConfig(); }
          });
        });

        // Menu Buttons
        document.getElementById('btn-single').addEventListener('click', () => {
          document.getElementById('menu-modes').style.display = 'none';
          document.getElementById('menu-difficulty').style.display = 'block';
        });

        document.getElementById('btn-host').addEventListener('click', e => initOnlineHost(e.currentTarget));

        document.getElementById('btn-diff-back').addEventListener('click', () => {
          document.getElementById('menu-difficulty').style.display = 'none';
          document.getElementById('menu-modes').style.display = 'block';
        });

        const joinSection = document.getElementById('join-section');
        const joinInput = document.getElementById('input-join-code');
        joinInput.addEventListener('input', () => {
          joinInput.value = joinInput.value.toUpperCase();
          joinSection.classList.remove('error');
        });

        document.getElementById('btn-join').addEventListener('click', () => {
          const code = joinInput.value.trim().toUpperCase();
          if (code.length < 3) {
            showToast('Please enter a valid code', 2);
            joinSection.classList.remove('error'); void joinSection.offsetWidth; joinSection.classList.add('error');
            joinInput.focus();
            return;
          }
          initOnlineClient(code);
        });

        joinInput.addEventListener('keydown', (e) => {
          if (e.key === 'Enter') document.getElementById('btn-join').click();
        });

        // Difficulty Select for Single Player
        document.querySelectorAll('.diff-btn').forEach(b => {
          b.addEventListener('click', () => {
            S.roomConfig.diff = b.dataset.diff;
            S.roomConfig.lives = 2;
            startMatch('single');
          });
        });

        // UI Interactions
        document.getElementById('btn-mute').addEventListener('click', e => { S.muted = !S.muted; e.currentTarget.textContent = S.muted ? '🔇' : '🔊'; });
        document.getElementById('btn-pause').addEventListener('click', togglePause);
        document.getElementById('btn-resume').addEventListener('click', togglePause);

        ['btn-restart', 'btn-playagain'].forEach(id => {
          const el = document.getElementById(id); if (el) el.addEventListener('click', () => { hideOverlays(); startMatch(S.mode); });
        });
        ['btn-mainmenu', 'btn-mainmenu2', 'btn-lobby-leave'].forEach(id => {
          const el = document.getElementById(id); if (el) el.addEventListener('click', backToMenu);
        });

        const btnCopy = document.getElementById('btn-copy-code');
        if (btnCopy) btnCopy.addEventListener('click', () => {
          if (!S.currentRoomCode) return;
          try { navigator.clipboard.writeText(S.currentRoomCode).catch(() => { }); } catch (e) { }
          btnCopy.textContent = 'Copied!'; setTimeout(() => btnCopy.textContent = 'Copy Code', 1500);
        });

        document.getElementById('btn-start-game').addEventListener('click', () => {
          if (S.playerOrder.length < 2) return; S.matchStarted = true; startMatch('multi');
        });

        bindJoystick(document.getElementById('joy-zone'), document.getElementById('joy-knob'));
        bindActionButton(document.getElementById('btn-action'));

        requestAnimationFrame(loop);
      });
    })();
  </script>
</body>

</html>
